# Daily video prompt pack — 28/10/2026 · Pig day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំកុរ)

Lucky signs: Pig, Rabbit, Goat · Careful: Snake
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.

## Luckiest animal today – Pig (កុរ) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color brown (ត្នោត) · best time 05:00–07:00 · lucky numbers 8 · 22 · 283 · 2588 · 56894 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of a chubby, adorable golden piglet with big happy eyes and rosy cheeks, the Pig of the Chinese zodiac, inspired by the charm and enchanting visual appeal of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. The pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune and a happy home: a happy, warm and generous expression and pose that show this. Body in creamy white-gold with shining engraved gold patterns, pink inner ears or rosy cheeks, polished gold textures, luminous highlights and delicate pink and gold accents. It sits on a round golden pedestal inside a large glowing pink lotus floating on a still pond. Behind it, a huge glowing full moon with a soft silver-gold halo, a deep indigo starry sky, a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with warm golden lights, palm tree silhouettes, small floating lanterns, glowing lotus flowers on the water, gentle moonbeams falling on the pig, magical sparkles and soft silver-and-gold reflections. Cinematic lighting, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, professional animated-movie-quality rendering. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around the character. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cropped body parts.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video about the Pig, today's luckiest animal: 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Pig as ingredients. Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 7, 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

★ = used to open this video. Why: Specific and true (only the day's animal has a Great Day), creates curiosity, and every viewer wants to know if it is their sign.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours? Today, Wednesday, 28 October 2026, is a Pig day.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Generous and warm-hearted, the Pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune, and a happy home. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Born in the Year of the Pig? Your luck is strong today. Stay happy, warm and generous. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Your lucky color today is brown. Wear it, or carry something brown with you. Your best time is from 5 to 7 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Your lucky direction today is the east. When you start something new, face the east, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
For lucky numbers: 8, 22, and 283. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 8, 22, 283!

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
My tip for the Year of the Pig: say yes to good chances, and lead with confidence. Snake friends, take it easy today.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)

The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Pig? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Wednesday, 28 October 2026, is a Pig day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Pig. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Generous and warm-hearted, the Pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune, and a happy home. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Pig are warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing. Pigs value comfort, kindness, and honest relationships.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Pig? Your luck is strong today. Stay happy, warm and generous. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
For love and family today, be warm and kind. A kind word, a smile, or a small gift can bring you closer to the people you love.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time. Finish what matters most first, and ask for help when you need it.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
For money, keep things simple and careful today. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is brown. Wear something brown, or carry a small brown item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 5 to 7 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the east. When you start something new, face the east, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 8, 22, and 283. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 8, 22, 283!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Pig's lucky days of the week are Thursday and Sunday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Pig can be overly trusting or indulgent. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: brown. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 8, 22, and 283.

សម្រាប់ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមានចិត្តកក់ក្ដៅ និងសប្បុរស។ ពាក្យសម្ដីល្អ ស្នាមញញឹម ឬអំណោយតូចមួយ អាចនាំឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែជិតស្និទ្ធ នឹងមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់។

សម្រាប់ការងារ និងការសិក្សា សូមធ្វើម្ដងមួយជំហាន ឲ្យបានច្បាស់លាស់។ សូមធ្វើរឿងសំខាន់បំផុតមុនគេ ហើយកុំខ្មាសក្នុងការសុំជំនួយ ពេលអ្នកត្រូវការ។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
សម្រាប់លុយកាក់ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមចាយវាយឲ្យសាមញ្ញ និងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ សូមគិតគូរមុននឹងចាយ ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ត្នោត។ សូមស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌ត្នោត ឬយករបស់ពណ៌ត្នោតមួយ ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
ទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺទិសខាងកើត។ ពេលចាប់ផ្ដើមធ្វើអ្វីថ្មី សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសខាងកើត ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបី លេខម្ភៃពីរ និងលេខពីររយប៉ែតសិបបី។ សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ ប្រាំបី ម្ភៃពីរ ពីររយប៉ែតសិបបី!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ថ្ងៃសំណាងប្រចាំសប្ដាហ៍ របស់ឆ្នាំកុរ គឺថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ។ សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ជួនកាលជឿគេពេក ឬធ្វេសប្រហែសខ្លួនឯង។ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។ ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបី លេខម្ភៃពីរ និងលេខពីររយប៉ែតសិបបី។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)

00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 character pictures as ingredients (each sign's clips use its own picture). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### Topic and audience
A full daily fortune for all twelve Chinese zodiac signs. For anyone who wants their own sign's rating, lucky color, best time and direction; chapters let each viewer jump to their sign.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Curiosity
   English: Three signs are lucky today, and one should take it easy. Where does your sign stand?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានបីឆ្នាំដែលមានសំណាងល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំដែលគួរសម្រាក។ តើឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នក នៅត្រង់ណា?
2. Question
   English: Which of the twelve zodiac signs is the luckiest today?
   Khmer: តើរាសីណា ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ?
3. Practical
   English: Your lucky color, best time, and direction for today, for all twelve signs, in about five minutes.

   English: Whatever your sign, today has a lucky color and a best time just for you.
   Khmer: មិនថាអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វីទេ ថ្ងៃនេះមានពណ៌សំណាង និងពេលល្អ សម្រាប់អ្នក។
5. Daily fortune
   English: Today is a Pig day. Here's what that means for every sign.

★ = used to open this video. Why: Every viewer has a sign in this video, so this gives everyone a reason to keep watching until their chapter.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
ឆ្នាំកុរ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នក។ ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក គឺពណ៌ត្នោត ហើយពេលល្អបំផុត គឺម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ និងធ្វើអ្វីៗដោយទំនុកចិត្ត។ ទិសសំណាង គឺទិសខាងកើត។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខម្ភៃពីរ និងលេខពីររយប៉ែតសិបបី។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)

Scene 31 (5:00–5:10)

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Pig and the Moon Lantern" (ជ្រូកមាស និងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden pig and Kong the little frog must bring the light back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Kong once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Pig picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Kong and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Kong the little frog (make once, keep it)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of Kong, a tiny cute green-and-gold frog with big sparkling eyes, a wide happy smile and a little lotus-leaf hat, inspired by the charm of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. Adorable expressive face, warm magical glow, on a glowing pink lotus by a pond at night, a softly lit Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat and a full moon behind. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Once upon a time
1:20 The lucky color
1:40 The lucky path
2:30 The stone door
3:40 The full-moon festival
4:30 Today's luck

### Topic and audience
An original animated bedtime story starring the Pig, with today's lucky color, direction and numbers in the plot. For families, children and zodiac fans who enjoy gentle stories.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Story question
   English: Can one little golden pig bring the light back before the full moon rises?
   Khmer: តើជ្រូកមាសតូចមួយ អាចនាំពន្លឺត្រឡប់មកវិញ មុនព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់រះ បានទេ?
2. Mystery
   English: On the night of the full moon, something at the temple went wrong. Only one little hero could fix it.
   Khmer: នៅយប់ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ មានរឿងមួយមិនប្រក្រតី នៅឯប្រាសាទ។ មានតែវីរបុរសតូចម្នាក់ប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលអាចជួយបាន។
3. Identity
   English: Born in the Year of the Pig? This bedtime story is about you.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរមែនទេ? រឿងនិទាននេះ គឺនិយាយអំពីអ្នក។
4. Challenge
   English: Three lucky treasures. One hard challenge. One golden pig. Can it make it in time?
   Khmer: រតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី។ ឧបសគ្គមួយ។ ជ្រូកមាសមួយ។ តើវាទាន់ពេលឬទេ?
5. Practical
   English: Today's lucky color, direction, and numbers, told as a magical story.
   Khmer: ពណ៌ ទិស និងលេខសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ ក្នុងរឿងនិទានដ៏អស្ចារ្យមួយ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Opens on the story's real question, which the ending answers, and the hero is today's luckiest animal.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. ## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden pig bring the light back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the moon lantern grew dim. Its light was almost gone. Without it, tonight's full-moon festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden pig. Today was a Pig day, and the pig felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little frog named Kong came hopping over. "Little Pig," he said, "only you can bring the light back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Generous and warm-hearted, the Pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune, and a happy home. Kong smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To relight the moon lantern, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the pig, and it set off on its adventure, with Kong right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Pig are warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing. Pigs value comfort, kindness, and honest relationships.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was brown, the pig's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The pig tied a brown ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Kong, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the east, the pig's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But a wide river blocked the path, with only small stepping stones to cross it. The pig stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Pig can be overly trusting or indulgent.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Kong came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Stone by stone, the pig hopped across the river. Each stone glowed gold under its feet. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Kong. For the Pig today, that is from 5 to 7 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the pig rang the golden bells 8 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The pig climbed 22 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a shining pearl of moonlight. It was the heart of the moon lantern!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the pearl, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Thursday and Sunday shone brightest, the Pig's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the pearl carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Kong right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The pig placed the pearl inside the moon lantern. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! A golden light burst into the sky!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The lotus pond glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The pig felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Pig, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: brown. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 8, 22, and 283.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Kong fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ជ្រូកលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។ ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។ នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
គង់និយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់ជ្រូកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ជ្រូកវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនប្រាំបី ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ជ្រូកឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនម្ភៃពីរ កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគុជពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគុជ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំកុរ។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គុជយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ជ្រូកដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ជ្រូកមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

"

55
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,011
ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ជ្រូកលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។

56
00:02:13,061 --> 00:02:17,195
ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។

57
00:02:17,245 --> 00:02:19,950
នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

58
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

59
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

60
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

61
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

62
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

63
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

64
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

65
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,563
គង់និយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

66
00:02:41,613 --> 00:02:44,385
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

67
00:02:44,435 --> 00:02:46,079
" សម្រាប់ជ្រូកថ្ងៃនេះ

68
00:02:46,129 --> 00:02:48,095
គឺម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

69
00:02:48,145 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

70
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,897
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ជ្រូកវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

71
00:02:52,947 --> 00:02:56,687
ចំនួន 8 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

72
00:02:56,737 --> 00:02:57,950
ទីង!

73
00:02:58,000 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

74
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,224
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

75
00:03:03,274 --> 00:03:05,968
ជ្រូកឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 22 កាំ

76
00:03:06,018 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

77
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,768
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

78
00:03:11,818 --> 00:03:16,541
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគុជពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង។

79
00:03:16,591 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ!

80
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,142
នៅជុំវិញគុជ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ

81
00:03:22,192 --> 00:03:23,169
ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

82
00:03:23,219 --> 00:03:25,429
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

83
00:03:25,479 --> 00:03:27,416
ថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ

84
00:03:27,466 --> 00:03:29,950
ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំកុរ។

85
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,721
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

86
00:03:31,771 --> 00:03:35,367
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គុជយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

87
00:03:35,417 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរ។

88
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

89
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

90
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

91
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,315
ជ្រូកដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។

92
00:03:53,365 --> 00:03:56,008
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

93
00:03:56,058 --> 00:03:57,354
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

94
00:03:57,404 --> 00:03:59,950
ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

95
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,491
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។

96
00:04:02,541 --> 00:04:03,639
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

97
00:04:03,689 --> 00:04:06,835
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

98
00:04:06,885 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

99
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,095
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

100
00:04:13,145 --> 00:04:14,950
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

101
00:04:15,000 --> 00:04:17,853
ជ្រូកមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

102
00:04:17,903 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

103
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

104
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

105
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

106
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

107
00:04:31,680 --> 00:04:33,150
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។

108
00:04:33,200 --> 00:04:35,630
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

109
00:04:35,680 --> 00:04:37,230
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

110
00:04:37,280 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 8 លេខ 22 និងលេខ 283។

111
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,155
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

112
00:04:44,205 --> 00:04:47,450
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងគង់ ក៏លង់លក់

113
00:04:47,500 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

114
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

115
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

116
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

117
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

118
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-28-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,778
Can one little golden pig bring the light

2
00:00:02,828 --> 00:00:04,984
back before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,034 --> 00:00:07,881
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,931 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,240
But this morning

6
00:00:11,290 --> 00:00:13,337
the moon lantern grew dim.

7
00:00:13,387 --> 00:00:15,434
Its light was almost gone.

8
00:00:15,484 --> 00:00:17,934
Without it, tonight's full-moon

9
00:00:17,984 --> 00:00:19,950
festival could not begin.

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,965
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

11
00:00:23,015 --> 00:00:25,979
opened, and out came a little golden pig.

12
00:00:26,029 --> 00:00:27,376
Today was a Pig day

13
00:00:27,426 --> 00:00:29,950
and the pig felt something special.

14
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,495
A little frog named

15
00:00:31,545 --> 00:00:33,365
Kong came hopping over.

16
00:00:33,415 --> 00:00:35,072
"Little Pig," he said

17
00:00:35,122 --> 00:00:37,917
"only you can bring the light back.

18
00:00:37,967 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,749
Generous and warm-hearted

20
00:00:41,799 --> 00:00:43,763
the Pig symbolizes abundance

21
00:00:43,813 --> 00:00:45,993
good fortune, and a happy home.

22
00:00:46,043 --> 00:00:46,856
Kong smiled.

23
00:00:46,906 --> 00:00:48,295
That was exactly why

24
00:00:48,345 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

25
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,110
To relight the moon lantern

26
00:00:52,160 --> 00:00:54,350
the little hero needed three

27
00:00:54,400 --> 00:00:56,910
lucky treasures: its lucky color

28
00:00:56,960 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

29
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,842
"Let's go!" said the pig

30
00:01:02,892 --> 00:01:06,577
and it set off on its adventure

31
00:01:06,627 --> 00:01:09,950
with Kong right by its side.

32
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,586
People born in the Year of the Pig

33
00:01:12,636 --> 00:01:15,841
are warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing.

34
00:01:15,891 --> 00:01:17,237
Pigs value comfort

35
00:01:17,287 --> 00:01:19,950
kindness, and honest relationships.

36
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,223
The first treasure was waiting

37
00:01:22,273 --> 00:01:24,874
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

38
00:01:24,924 --> 00:01:26,768
Only one shone brightest.

39
00:01:26,818 --> 00:01:29,950
It was brown, the pig's lucky color today.

40
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,736
The pig tied a brown

41
00:01:31,786 --> 00:01:33,789
ribbon around its neck.

42
00:01:33,839 --> 00:01:36,825
"Wear your lucky color," said Kong

43
00:01:36,875 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

44
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,577
Next, they came to a crossroads

45
00:01:42,627 --> 00:01:43,933
with many paths.

46
00:01:43,983 --> 00:01:45,882
One path began to glow.

47
00:01:45,932 --> 00:01:47,747
It pointed to the east

48
00:01:47,797 --> 00:01:49,950
the pig's lucky direction.

49
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,858
But a wide river blocked the path

50
00:01:51,908 --> 00:01:53,245
with only small stepping

51
00:01:53,295 --> 00:01:54,343
stones to cross it.

52
00:01:54,393 --> 00:01:55,268
The pig stopped.

53
00:01:55,318 --> 00:01:57,638
Sometimes, people born in the Year of the

54
00:01:57,688 --> 00:01:59,950
Pig can be overly trusting or indulgent.

55
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,546
Kong came close and smiled.

56
00:02:02,596 --> 00:02:05,046
"Breathe slowly," he said.

57
00:02:05,096 --> 00:02:06,777
"Trust your heart.

58
00:02:06,827 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

59
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,805
Stone by stone, the pig

60
00:02:11,855 --> 00:02:13,740
hopped across the river.

61
00:02:13,790 --> 00:02:16,805
Each stone glowed gold under its feet.

62
00:02:16,855 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

63
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

64
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

67
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

68
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

69
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

70
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,563
"The door opens only

71
00:02:41,613 --> 00:02:43,982
at your best time," said Kong.

72
00:02:44,032 --> 00:02:45,353
For the Pig today

73
00:02:45,403 --> 00:02:48,176
that is from 5 to 7 in the morning.

74
00:02:48,226 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

75
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,529
When the time came

76
00:02:51,579 --> 00:02:54,775
the pig rang the golden bells 8 times

77
00:02:54,825 --> 00:02:56,792
its first lucky number.

78
00:02:56,842 --> 00:02:57,845
Ding!

79
00:02:57,895 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

80
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,104
The door opened

81
00:03:01,154 --> 00:03:03,642
and a stairway of light appeared.

82
00:03:03,692 --> 00:03:06,104
The pig climbed 22 glowing steps

83
00:03:06,154 --> 00:03:07,873
its second lucky number

84
00:03:07,923 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

85
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:12,250
At the top of the tower

86
00:03:12,300 --> 00:03:16,250
they found a shining pearl of moonlight.

87
00:03:16,300 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the moon lantern!

88
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,753
Around the pearl, seven little lights

89
00:03:22,803 --> 00:03:25,708
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

90
00:03:25,758 --> 00:03:28,359
Thursday and Sunday shone brightest

91
00:03:28,409 --> 00:03:29,950
the Pig's lucky days.

92
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,009
Time was running out.

93
00:03:32,059 --> 00:03:34,656
Holding the pearl carefully

94
00:03:34,706 --> 00:03:37,401
the little hero hurried home

95
00:03:37,451 --> 00:03:39,950
with Kong right beside it.

96
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

97
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

98
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

99
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

100
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,855
The pig placed the pearl

101
00:03:51,905 --> 00:03:53,760
inside the moon lantern.

102
00:03:53,810 --> 00:03:56,220
For a moment, nothing happened.

103
00:03:56,270 --> 00:03:57,252
Then, whoosh!

104
00:03:57,302 --> 00:03:59,950
A golden light burst into the sky!

105
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,104
The lotus pond glowed again.

106
00:04:02,154 --> 00:04:04,104

fortune for today.

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Snake (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Snake, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Snake clashes with the Pig day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Snake as ingredients (the Snake is the only animal in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the snake hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little lotus lantern keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

Is your sign the one that should take it easy today? Today, 28 October, is a slower day for the Year of the Snake.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Pig day, and the Snake sits opposite it in the zodiac circle. Some days, things just don't go the way we planned.

And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Be careful today: you can be secretive or prone to overthinking. Notice it, and give yourself a moment before you react.

Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

តើរាសីរបស់អ្នក ជារាសីដែលគួរសម្រាកថ្ងៃនេះឬទេ? ថ្ងៃទីម្ភៃប្រាំបី ខែតុលា ជាថ្ងៃដែលអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ គួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំកុរ ហើយឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ស្ថិតនៅទល់មុខ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។ មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដើរតាមផែនការរបស់យើងទេ។

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។ មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។ ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។ សូមកត់សម្គាល់វា ហើយផ្ដល់ពេលឲ្យខ្លួនឯងបន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់ និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។ ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់ សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។ រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
យប់នេះ សូមសម្រាកឲ្យបានល្អ។ ថ្ងៃស្អែក ជាថ្ងៃថ្មី ហើយអាចជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមតាមដាន MyBirthSign ដើម្បីដឹងជោគជតារបស់អ្នករៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ!

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Premium 3D animated fantasy film quality, inspired by classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films: cute and majestic golden zodiac characters with oversized sparkling eyes, adorable expressive faces, soft rounded features, polished gold textures with intricate engraved ornaments and delicate pink and gold accents, alive and emotionally expressive. Setting: a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under a glowing full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, floating lotus flowers, ornate pink-and-gold lotus details, magical sparkles and warm golden reflections. Cinematic lighting, rich colors, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, gentle slow camera movement, professional animated-movie rendering. The zodiac animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos.

## Reference pictures (make these first, so each animal looks the same in every scene)
Optional ready-made references: your site's golden animal pictures, e.g. https://mybirthsign.com/images/profile/spirit-tiger.webp. Make each picture once, then add it as a reference image ("ingredient") in Flow, or use it as the starting image of a scene.

### Character reference image prompts
- **Chanda (ចន្ទា), the presenter (make once, use in every video):** Create a premium-quality 3D animated character, inspired by the charm and warmth of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films: Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel. Expressive, kind face, graceful and confident posture, one hand held open toward the temple as if presenting it, the other resting at her waist. She stands on a carved sandstone temple terrace with an ornate stone balustrade, beside a lotus pond with glowing pink lotus flowers; behind her, Angkor Wat glows golden and reflects in the water, under a deep purple night sky with a huge glowing full moon and floating sky lanterns. ## YouTube subtitles (captions files)
Two files are saved next to this one: `2026-10-28.srt` (Khmer) and `2026-10-28.en.srt` (English).
Upload in YouTube Studio → Subtitles → Add language → choose Khmer or English → Upload file → "With timing". You can add both to the same video.
The timings are estimates for a 45-second voice-over; after you record the voice, drag each line to match in YouTube's subtitle editor, or use "Auto-sync".
